<script>
const words=[

['make a good / bad / lasting first impression','Tạo ấn tượng tốt / khó quên ngay lần gặp đầu tiên','make a good first impression','People & Relationships','When I went to my job interview, I wanted to make a good first impression.'],
['come out of my shell','Bớt rụt rè, trở nên cởi mở hơn','slowly come out of my shell','People & Relationships','When I started university, I slowly came out of my shell.'],
['a bit of an introvert','Hơi hướng nội','be an introvert; a bit of an introvert','People & Relationships','I used to be a bit of an introvert, but I gradually became more confident.'],
['a role model','Hình mẫu để noi theo','a role model for someone; look up to someone','People & Relationships','My English teacher was a real role model for me.'],
['set a good example','Làm gương tốt','set a good / bad example to someone','People & Relationships','She always set a good example by staying calm.'],
['bring out the best in someone','Giúp ai đó phát huy những điểm tốt nhất','bring out the best in me / someone','People & Relationships','My best friend brings out the best in me.'],
['an extended family','Gia đình nhiều thế hệ','three generations living under one roof','People & Relationships','It was normal for three generations to live under one roof.'],
['a nuclear family','Gia đình hạt nhân','nuclear families are becoming more common','People & Relationships','Nuclear families are becoming more common in big cities.'],
['live under one roof','Sống chung dưới một mái nhà','live together under one roof','People & Relationships','Three generations often lived under one roof.'],
['go through a rebellious phase','Trải qua giai đoạn nổi loạn','go through a difficult / awkward phase','People & Relationships','I went through a rebellious phase when I was fifteen.'],
['respect your elders','Kính trọng người lớn tuổi','teach children to respect their elders','People & Relationships','Children are taught to respect their elders from a young age.'],
['a wealth of experience','Vốn kinh nghiệm phong phú','have a wealth of experience','People & Relationships','Older people have a wealth of experience.'],
['in the public eye','Được công chúng chú ý','live / grow up in the public eye','People & Relationships','Living in the public eye put her under pressure.'],
['a household name','Cái tên mà hầu như ai cũng biết','become a household name','People & Relationships','She became a household name around the world.'],
['strike up a conversation','Bắt chuyện với ai đó','strike up a conversation with someone','People & Relationships','I struck up a conversation with a girl online.'],
['meet someone in person','Gặp ai đó trực tiếp ngoài đời','meet in person for the first time','People & Relationships','We chatted online before we finally met in person.'],
["I wouldn't say I'm…, but I'm definitely…",'Tôi không hẳn là…, nhưng chắc chắn là…','I wouldn’t say I’m + adjective, but…','People & Relationships','I wouldn’t say I’m very outgoing, but I’m definitely a good listener.'],
['a hidden gem','Một địa điểm đẹp, thú vị nhưng ít người biết','a real hidden gem','Places','There’s a little café tucked away in an alley. It’s a real hidden gem.'],
['(be) well worth a visit','Rất đáng để ghé thăm','well worth a visit / a look / the trip','Places','The Vietnam Museum of Ethnology is well worth a visit.'],
['well worth + V-ing','Rất đáng để làm gì','well worth visiting / seeing / trying','Places','It’s well worth going early, before it gets crowded.'],
['spacious and airy','Rộng rãi và thoáng đãng','a spacious living room; a bright, airy room','Places','I’d like my dream home to be airy and bright.'],
['cramped','Chật chội, bí bách','a cramped flat; dark and cramped','Places','I don’t like dark and cramped rooms.'],
['plenty of natural light','Có nhiều ánh sáng tự nhiên','get / let in plenty of natural light','Places','I’d like my home to get plenty of natural light.'],
['feel at home','Cảm thấy thoải mái như ở nhà','make someone feel at home','Places','The staff always remember my order, so I feel at home there.'],
['a home away from home','Nơi thân thuộc như ở nhà','make somewhere feel like a home away from home','Places','My favourite café is a home away from home.'],
['a quiet spot','Một góc / địa điểm yên tĩnh','a quiet spot by the window','Places','I go to the library because it’s easier to concentrate.'],
['curl up with a book','Cuộn mình thoải mái để đọc sách','curl up on the sofa / in bed with a book','Places','I love curling up with a book on rainy days.'],
['a bustling market','Khu chợ đông vui, nhộn nhịp','a bustling local market / street','Places','I’d rather go to a bustling local market than a mall.'],
['haggle over the price','Mặc cả, trả giá','haggle with the seller over the price','Places','You can sometimes haggle with the sellers.'],
['unspoilt','Hoang sơ, ít bị tác động','an unspoilt beach / island / village','Places','I’d love to visit an unspoilt beach.'],
['crystal-clear water','Nước trong vắt','the water is crystal clear','Places','The beach has crystal-clear water.'],
['a slower pace of life','Nhịp sống chậm hơn','enjoy a slower pace of life','Places','People in the countryside enjoy a slower pace of life.'],
['laid-back','Thong thả, thoải mái','a laid-back lifestyle / atmosphere','Places','People there seem much more laid-back.'],
['a far cry from','Khác xa so với…','be a far cry from…','Places','Life in the countryside is a far cry from life in Hanoi.'],
['let off steam','Xả năng lượng, giải tỏa căng thẳng','let off steam after school / work','Places','Children need somewhere to run around and let off steam.'],
['child-friendly','Thân thiện, phù hợp với trẻ em','child-friendly parks / places / restaurants','Places','Big cities need more child-friendly parks.'],
['The first thing that strikes you about… is…','Điều đầu tiên khiến bạn chú ý về… là…','What strikes me most about… is…','Places','The first thing that strikes you about Hanoi is the noise.'],
['a rewarding job','Công việc ý nghĩa, đáng làm','a rewarding job / career / experience','Work & Study','Teaching is rewarding because you can see your students improve.'],
['job satisfaction','Sự hài lòng với công việc','job satisfaction matters','Work & Study','For me, job satisfaction matters more than a high salary.'],
['find something fulfilling','Cảm thấy việc gì đó có ý nghĩa','find + object + adjective','Work & Study','I find teaching fulfilling because I can help students improve.'],
['climb the career ladder','Thăng tiến trong sự nghiệp','climb the career ladder quickly','Work & Study','I’m not in a hurry to climb the career ladder.'],
['get promoted','Được thăng chức','get promoted to + position','Work & Study','She hopes to get promoted to manager one day.'],
['work my way up','Dần đi lên từ vị trí thấp','work my way up from A to B','Work & Study','I want to work my way up to become a full-time teacher.'],
['soft skills','Kỹ năng mềm','communication and teamwork','Work & Study','Many graduates struggle because they lack soft skills.'],
['transferable skills','Kỹ năng áp dụng trong nhiều công việc','transferable skills such as communication','Work & Study','Communication and teamwork are important transferable skills.'],
['be in high demand','Đang có nhu cầu cao, được săn đón','skills / workers are in high demand','Work & Study','People who speak good English are in high demand.'],
['vocational training','Đào tạo nghề','do vocational training','Work & Study','Vocational training helps young people learn practical skills.'],
['learn a trade','Học một nghề cụ thể','learn a trade like plumbing','Work & Study','Young people can learn a trade and develop practical skills.'],
["a degree isn't everything",'Bằng đại học không phải là tất cả','a university degree','Work & Study','Many parents think their children must go to university, but a degree isn’t everything.'],
['have butterflies in my stomach','Cảm thấy hồi hộp, bồn chồn','before an important event','Work & Study','Before my first presentation, I had butterflies in my stomach.'],
['think on my feet','Suy nghĩ và phản ứng nhanh','be good at thinking on my feet','Work & Study','I had to think on my feet when classmates asked unexpected questions.'],
['Looking back, I wish I had + V3','Nhìn lại, tôi ước mình đã…','I wish I had started earlier','Work & Study','Looking back, I wish I had started practising earlier.'],
['go the extra mile','Cố gắng thêm, làm hơn mức yêu cầu','go the extra mile for someone','Work & Study','My teacher always went the extra mile to help us.'],
['meet / miss a deadline','Kịp hạn / trễ hạn','work to a tight deadline','Work & Study','We had a tight deadline but managed to meet it.'],
['cram for an exam','Học nhồi nhét trước kỳ thi','last-minute cramming; pull an all-nighter','Work & Study','I used to cram the night before exams.'],
['work remotely / work from home','Làm việc từ xa / tại nhà','blur the line between work and home','Work & Study','Working from home can blur the line between work and home.'],
['hectic','Bận rộn, tất bật','have a hectic schedule','Daily Life','Things have got hectic since I started my new job.'],
['be pressed for time','Không có nhiều thời gian','when I’m pressed for time','Daily Life','When I’m pressed for time, I grab breakfast on the way.'],
['on the go','Bận suốt ngày, làm gì đó khi di chuyển','be on the go all day','Daily Life','I listen to podcasts on the go while commuting.'],
['run errands','Làm việc vặt','run errands for someone','Daily Life','I usually run errands at the weekend.'],
['do my fair share','Làm phần việc công bằng của mình','pull my weight','Daily Life','Everyone should do their fair share of the housework.'],
['squeeze something into my routine','Sắp xếp việc gì vào thói quen','squeeze in a quick workout','Daily Life','I try to squeeze a 20-minute walk into my routine.'],
['be stuck in a rut','Mắc kẹt trong lối sống nhàm chán','get out of a rut; shake things up','Daily Life','I felt stuck in a rut, so I decided to shake things up.'],
['These days…, whereas back in the day…','Ngày nay…, trong khi ngày xưa…','present simple / past simple','Daily Life','These days people order food through apps, whereas back in the day families cooked at home.'],
['get hooked on something','Bị cuốn hút, mê một thứ gì đó','get completely hooked on','Hobbies & Leisure','I got hooked on a drama and watched the whole series.'],
['a die-hard fan','Fan cứng, người hâm mộ trung thành','a die-hard fan of a team','Hobbies & Leisure','My dad is a die-hard football fan.'],
['have two left feet','Nhảy rất vụng về','be hopeless at dancing','Hobbies & Leisure','I have two left feet, so I’d rather sing than dance.'],
['let my hair down','Thả lỏng, vui chơi hết mình','let my hair down at karaoke','Hobbies & Leisure','At karaoke with friends, I can really let my hair down.'],
['be good with my hands','Khéo tay','take on a DIY project','Hobbies & Leisure','I’m not especially good with my hands, but I enjoy small DIY projects.'],
['on the big screen','Trên màn ảnh rộng','be worth seeing on the big screen','Hobbies & Leisure','I’d rather watch action films on the big screen.'],
['keep someone on the edge of their seat','Khiến ai hồi hộp, nín thở theo dõi','keep me on the edge of my seat','Hobbies & Leisure','The film kept me on the edge of my seat.'],
['a sore loser','Người thua không chịu được','be a good / bad loser','Hobbies & Leisure','My younger cousin is a bit of a sore loser.'],
['build up a collection','Dần xây dựng một bộ sưu tập','take pride in my collection','Hobbies & Leisure','I built up a collection of cartoon stickers.'],
['be spoilt for choice','Có quá nhiều lựa chọn tốt','be spoilt for choice when it comes to','Hobbies & Leisure','You’re spoilt for choice when it comes to cafés in the city centre.'],
['recharge my batteries','Nạp lại năng lượng, nghỉ ngơi','a chance to recharge my batteries','Hobbies & Leisure','I spend Sunday morning in a café to recharge my batteries.'],
["isn't as popular as it used to be",'Không còn phổ biến như trước','isn’t as popular as it used to be because…','Hobbies & Leisure','Going to the cinema isn’t as popular as it used to be.'],
['follow a recipe','Nấu theo công thức','follow a recipe step by step','Food & Lifestyle','I look up a recipe online and follow it step by step.'],
['a dab hand at','Rất khéo, rất giỏi việc gì','a dab hand at cooking','Food & Lifestyle','My mum is a real dab hand at making spring rolls.'],
['comfort food','Món ăn đem lại cảm giác ấm áp','my ultimate comfort food','Food & Lifestyle','Rice porridge is my ultimate comfort food.'],
['a staple of the Vietnamese diet','Món ăn chủ yếu của người Việt','rice is a staple food','Food & Lifestyle','Rice is a staple of the Vietnamese diet.'],
['No occasion is complete without…','Dịp nào đó sẽ không trọn vẹn nếu thiếu…','No + occasion + is complete without…','Food & Lifestyle','No Tết is complete without bánh chưng.'],
['go vegetarian / cut out meat','Chuyển sang ăn chay / loại bỏ thịt','cut out meat or sugar','Food & Lifestyle','Recently I’ve cut out fizzy drinks.'],
["can't function without my morning coffee",'Không thể hoạt động bình thường nếu thiếu cà phê','need a caffeine boost','Food & Lifestyle','Lots of young people can’t function without their morning coffee.'],
['order in / order a takeaway','Gọi đồ ăn về nhà / gọi món mang về','food delivery apps','Food & Lifestyle','When it’s pouring down, I’d rather order in.'],
['attentive staff / a bit of a rip-off','Nhân viên chu đáo / hơi đắt đỏ','friendly and attentive staff','Food & Lifestyle','The staff were attentive, but the drinks were a bit of a rip-off.'],
['There has been a shift towards…','Đã có sự chuyển dịch sang…','a shift towards + noun / V-ing','Food & Lifestyle','There has been a shift towards fast food among young people.'],
['miss out on something','Bỏ lỡ cơ hội, trải nghiệm','miss out on + noun / V-ing','Universal Words','Young people are afraid of missing out on what their friends are doing.'],
['be torn between A and B','Phân vân giữa hai lựa chọn','be torn between A and B','Universal Words','Many graduates are torn between staying near family and moving for work.'],
['be in two minds about something','Còn lưỡng lự, chưa quyết định','be in two minds about + noun / V-ing','Universal Words','I’m in two minds about whether to move to another city.'],
['take something into account','Cân nhắc, tính đến điều gì','take + object + into account','Universal Words','The government should take local families’ needs into account.'],
['become the norm','Trở thành điều bình thường, phổ biến','become the norm','Universal Words','Paying by QR code has become the norm in Vietnam.'],
['give something a go','Thử làm điều gì','give it a try / give it a shot','Universal Words','I’d love to give the ukulele a go.'],
['To be fair,…','Công bằng mà nói…','To be fair, though, + clause','Universal Words','To be fair, its clothes tend to last longer.'],
['get into something','Bắt đầu thích, say mê điều gì','get really into something','Universal Words','I only got into reading quite recently.'],
['pick something up','Học được một cách tự nhiên','pick up a language / skill','Universal Words','Working in retail helped me pick up customer-service skills.'],
['keep up with something','Theo kịp, bắt kịp','keep up with + noun','Universal Words','Older people sometimes find it hard to keep up with technology.'],
['have a lot on your plate','Có nhiều việc phải lo','have too much on my plate','Universal Words','I’ve got a lot on my plate with assignments and part-time work.'],
['make a (big) difference','Tạo ra sự khác biệt','make a real / big difference to something','Universal Words','Small habits can make a real difference to the environment.'],
["There's no point (in) doing something",'Chẳng có ích gì khi làm gì','There’s no point in + V-ing','Universal Words','There’s no point going to a famous restaurant if the food isn’t hygienic.'],
['end up doing something','Cuối cùng lại làm gì ngoài dự định','end up + V-ing','Universal Words','I intended to watch one episode but ended up watching five.'],
['catch up on something','Làm bù, cập nhật việc còn thiếu','catch up on sleep / homework','Universal Words','I usually catch up on sleep on Sunday mornings.'],
['catch up with someone','Gặp lại và trò chuyện sau một thời gian','catch up with friends / family','Universal Words','I often catch up with my university friends.'],
["can't help doing something",'Không thể nhịn được, không thể không','can’t help + V-ing','Universal Words','I can’t help comparing myself to people online.'],
['broaden your horizons','Mở rộng tầm nhìn, hiểu biết','broaden someone’s horizons','Universal Words','Working with tourists would broaden my horizons.'],
['strike a balance between A and B','Cân bằng giữa hai điều','strike a balance between A and B','Universal Words','Parents need to balance protection and freedom.'],
['fall behind','Tụt lại, chậm tiến độ','fall behind with homework / studies','Universal Words','I worried I’d fall behind with my IELTS practice.'],
['take something in your stride','Bình thản đón nhận, xử lý điều gì','take criticism / setbacks in your stride','Universal Words','Experienced travellers take flight delays in their stride.'],
['put something into practice','Áp dụng vào thực tế','put knowledge / ideas into practice','Universal Words','The job let me put my university knowledge into practice.'],
['get a lot out of something','Thu được nhiều điều, học hỏi được nhiều','get a lot out of + noun / V-ing','Universal Words','I get a lot out of reading.'],
['be worth doing something','Đáng để làm gì','be worth + V-ing','Universal Words','It’s well worth trying bún chả in Hanoi.'],
['bring back memories','Gợi lại kỷ niệm','bring back memories of + noun','Universal Words','The smell of the sea brings back memories of Nha Trang.'],
['get by','Xoay xở được, sống tạm được','get by on / with','Universal Words','Lots of students get by on very little money.'],
['raise awareness','Nâng cao nhận thức','raise awareness of / about something','Universal Words','Schools can run campaigns to raise awareness of plastic waste.'],
['have a knack for something','Có khiếu, có biệt tài','have a knack for + V-ing','Universal Words','A good tour guide has a knack for making strangers feel relaxed.'],
['hit-and-miss','Lúc được lúc không, chất lượng thất thường','be a bit / quite hit-and-miss','Universal Words','AI translation is still quite hit-and-miss.'],
['The way I see it,…','Theo cách tôi nhìn nhận thì…','The way I see it, + clause','Universal Words','The way I see it, tourism is good for Hoi An if local families can afford to live there.']
];
words.push(...[["go about","tiếp tục hoặc bắt đầu làm một việc gì đó","go about your day / work","Daily Life","There is really nothing special about how I go about my day.","/ɡəʊ əˈbaʊt/"],["a bite to eat","một chút đồ ăn, món ăn nhẹ","grab / have a bite to eat","Daily Life","I only have time to grab a bite to eat before catching the bus.","/ə baɪt tuː iːt/"],["bound for","đang trên đường đến một nơi nào đó","a bus / train bound for + place","Daily Life","I caught the bus bound for the university.","/baʊnd fɔː/"],["fussy","cầu kỳ, kén chọn","be fussy about something","Daily Life","I am not fussy about my appearance, so casual clothes are all I need.","/ˈfʌs.i/"],["hit the hay","lên giường đi ngủ","hit the hay around midnight","Daily Life","I hit the hay around midnight and get ready to do it all again the next day.","/ˈhɪt ðə heɪ/"],["be snowed under","bị ngập đầu trong công việc","be snowed under with work","Weekend","I am often snowed under with work in the middle of the week.","/ˈbiː ˈsnəʊd ˈʌndə/"],["bundle up","mặc ấm, quấn nhiều lớp","bundle up in bed / warm clothes","Weekend","Sometimes I just want to bundle up in bed and take a well-deserved rest.","/ˈbʌndl ʌp/"],["gaze","nhìn ngắm chăm chú trong một lúc","gaze at something; gaze longingly","Weekend","On weekdays, I can only gaze longingly at the lush green zoo.","/ɡeɪz/"],["swarm with","đầy ắp, có rất nhiều thứ hoặc người","be swarming with people","Weekend","The zoo is always swarming with adults and children.","/swɔːm wɪð/"],["uplifting","làm vui lên, nâng cao tinh thần","an uplifting way to recharge","Weekend","It is an uplifting way to recharge on my days off.","/ʌpˈlɪf.tɪŋ/"],["obscure","ít người biết đến, khuất nẻo","a fairly obscure place","Travel","Thach Hai is a fairly obscure place, but it is beautiful.","/əbˈskjʊər/"],["anonymity","tình trạng ít được biết đến, nặc danh","relative anonymity","Travel","Despite its relative anonymity, hordes of people still visit the beach.","/ˌæn.ɒnˈɪm.ə.ti/"],["horde","đám đông, đoàn người đông đảo","hordes of people","Travel","Hordes of people still flock there every day.","/hɔːd/"],["transfixed","bị cuốn hút đến sững sờ","be transfixed by the view","Travel","I was wide awake and transfixed by the view at sunrise.","/trænsˈfɪkst/"],["the wee hours","khoảng thời gian sau nửa đêm, sáng sớm tinh mơ","in the wee hours","Travel","My parents woke me in the wee hours and drove me to the beach.","/ðə ˈwiː ˌaʊ.əz/"],["embark","bắt đầu, dấn thân vào một hành trình hoặc việc gì","embark on a trip / journey","Memorable Experiences","Four years ago, I embarked on a trip to Hanoi with some special people.","/ɪmˈbɑːk/"],["anecdote","câu chuyện ngắn về một sự việc có thật","a personal anecdote","Memorable Experiences","We talked about every topic, from personal anecdotes to professional subjects.","/ˈæn.ɪk.dəʊt/"],["glee","niềm vui sướng, hân hoan","with glee; feel glee","Memorable Experiences","I felt immense glee when I learned we could study and explore Hanoi together.","/ɡliː/"],["star-struck","kinh ngạc, choáng ngợp khi gặp người mình ngưỡng mộ","be star-struck by someone","Memorable Experiences","I was star-struck to study with brilliant seniors I deeply admired.","/ˈstɑː.strʌk/"],["the cherry on the cake","điều làm cho trải nghiệm vốn tốt trở nên hoàn hảo hơn","the cherry on the cake was…","Memorable Experiences","The cherry on the cake was exploring Hanoi with the whole team.","/ðə ˈtʃeri ɒn ðə keɪk/"],["incident","sự việc, sự cố (thường không mong muốn)","an incident; several incidents","Hobbies & Leisure","Those incidents never deterred me from painting.","/ˈɪn.sɪ.dənt/"],["come rain or shine","dù có chuyện gì xảy ra, bất kể thời tiết","come rain or shine","Hobbies & Leisure","I am sure art will stay with me, come rain or shine.","/kʌm reɪn ɔː ʃaɪn/"],["dabble","thử làm gì đó trong thời gian ngắn, cho vui","dabble in something","Hobbies & Leisure","I usually paint with watercolour, though I have also dabbled in gouache.","/ˈdæb.əl/"],["deter","ngăn cản, làm ai nhụt chí","deter someone from doing something","Hobbies & Leisure","Those incidents never deterred me from pursuing art.","/dɪˈtɜːr/"],["of choice","được chọn hoặc yêu thích nhất","a medium / activity of choice","Hobbies & Leisure","Watercolour is usually my medium of choice.","/əv tʃɔɪs/"],["down in the dumps","buồn bã, chán nản","feel / be down in the dumps","Love & Hate","When you are down in the dumps, affection can cheer you up.","/daʊn ɪn ðə dʌmps/"],["hinder","cản trở, gây khó khăn cho tiến triển","hinder someone / progress","Love & Hate","Scorn can hinder your progress, while affection propels you forward.","/ˈhɪn.dər/"],["in someone’s bad books","không được ai đó ưa, bị ai đó phật ý","be / end up in someone’s bad books","Love & Hate","A terrible first impression may put you in someone’s bad books.","/ɪn ˈsʌmwʌnz bæd bʊks/"],["propel","đẩy hoặc thúc đẩy tiến về phía trước","propel someone forward","Love & Hate","Affection can propel you forward in a positive direction.","/prəˈpel/"],["scorn","sự khinh miệt, căm ghét","show / feel scorn","Love & Hate","Scorn can be discouraging and hinder your progress.","/skɔːn/"],["shopaholic","người nghiện mua sắm","be a shopaholic; shopaholic tendencies","Shopping","Sue is a shopaholic, and the shopping mall is paradise to her.","/ˌʃɒp.əˈhɒl.ɪk/"],["designer","hàng hiệu, do nhà thiết kế nổi tiếng tạo ra","a designer bag / label","Shopping","She dreams of buying a designer bag and branded shoes.","/dɪˈzaɪ.nər/"],["frugal","tiết kiệm, tránh chi tiêu hoang phí","be frugal with money","Shopping","Sue knows she should be more frugal with her money.","/ˈfruː.ɡəl/"],["spree","cuộc vui hoặc đợt làm gì đó kéo dài, thường quá mức","a spending / shopping spree","Shopping","No fortune could survive one of her spending sprees.","/spriː/"],["window shopping","đi xem hàng mà không nhất thiết mua","go / do some window shopping","Shopping","When she cannot afford a new bag, she goes window shopping.","/ˈwɪn.dəʊ ˌʃɒp.ɪŋ/"],["dwindle","giảm dần về số lượng hoặc kích thước","dwindle rapidly / over time","Animals","Wildlife populations are dwindling rapidly because of poaching.","/ˈdwɪn.dəl/"],["complicit","đồng lõa, có liên quan đến việc sai trái","be complicit in something","Animals","By buying illegal animal products, consumers become complicit in creating demand.","/kəmˈplɪs.ɪt/"],["cruelty-free","không thử nghiệm hoặc gây hại cho động vật","cruelty-free products / cosmetics","Animals","You should support cruelty-free products and cosmetics.","/ˈkruːəlti friː/"],["poacher","kẻ săn bắt động vật trái phép","at the hands of poachers","Animals","Many wild creatures are dying at the hands of poachers.","/ˈpəʊ.tʃər/"],["traffic (verb)","buôn bán trái phép người hoặc động vật","traffic wildlife / animals","Animals","Wild animals are trafficked across many borders.","/ˈtræf.ɪk/"],["tapestry","tấm thảm; hình ảnh nhiều màu sắc đan xen","a tapestry of colours","Seasons","In autumn, the trees cover the ground with a tapestry of gold and brown.","/ˈtæp.ɪ.stri/"],["mellow","dịu nhẹ, êm ái; không gay gắt","a mellow breeze / light","Seasons","A mellow breeze carries the sweet scent of ripe fruit.","/ˈmel.əʊ/"],["persistent","dai dẳng, kéo dài không dứt","persistent rain / problems","Seasons","Autumn has no persistent rain like spring.","/pəˈsɪs.tənt/"],["scorching","nóng gay gắt, nóng như thiêu đốt","scorching sun / heat","Seasons","Autumn has no scorching sun like summer.","/ˈskɔː.tʃɪŋ/"],["smitten","say mê, yêu thích sâu sắc","be smitten with someone / something","Seasons","I am simply smitten with Halloween and autumn.","/ˈsmɪt.ən/"],["underrated","bị đánh giá thấp hơn giá trị thực","an underrated means of transport","Transport","In my opinion, the bus is an underrated means of transport.","/ˌʌn.dəˈreɪ.tɪd/"],["clog up","làm tắc nghẽn, bít kín","clog up the roads / drains","Transport","More buses could help reduce the vehicles that clog up city roads.","/klɒɡ ʌp/"],["compact (verb)","nén, ép gọn lại","compact people / commuters onto buses","Transport","If commuters are compacted onto buses, they take up less road space.","/kəmˈpækt/"],["exhaust fume","khí thải từ động cơ","exhaust fumes from vehicles","Transport","Passengers produce fewer exhaust fumes when they take public transport.","/ɪɡˈzɔːst fjuːm/"],["surplus","phần dư thừa, nhiều hơn mức cần thiết","a surplus of vehicles / goods","Transport","A surplus of vehicles on the road contributes to traffic congestion.","/ˈsɜː.pləs/"],["commodity","mặt hàng; tiện nghi hoặc dịch vụ thiết yếu","ready-made commodities","Rural & Urban","Cities offer ready-made commodities such as recreation and healthcare.","/kəˈmɒd.ə.ti/"],["recreation","hoạt động giải trí, thư giãn","recreation and healthcare","Rural & Urban","Cities offer recreation and healthcare within easy reach.","/ˌrek.riˈeɪ.ʃən/"],["tight-knit","gắn bó, thân thiết như một cộng đồng","a tight-knit community","Rural & Urban","The countryside has a much more tight-knit community than the city.","/ˌtaɪtˈnɪt/"],["tranquility","sự yên bình, tĩnh lặng","the tranquility of the countryside","Rural & Urban","The tranquility of the countryside is unparalleled.","/træŋˈkwɪl.ə.ti/"],["unparalleled","vô song, không gì sánh bằng","unparalleled beauty / tranquility","Rural & Urban","The countryside offers unparalleled peace and quiet.","/ʌnˈpær.əl.eld/"],["diet","chế độ ăn uống","a balanced / healthy diet","Health","A balanced diet and regular exercise are important for good health.","/ˈdaɪ.ət/"],["fibre","chất xơ; sợi","dietary fibre; a high-fibre diet","Health","Try to include dietary fibre in your everyday meals.","/ˈfaɪ.bər/"],["nutrient","chất dinh dưỡng cần thiết cho cơ thể","essential nutrients","Health","A healthy meal should include essential nutrients such as fibre and protein.","/ˈnjuː.tri.ənt/"],["regime","chế độ, kế hoạch được duy trì đều đặn","a fitness / exercise regime","Health","You can build a fitness regime with regular walking or cycling.","/reɪˈʒiːm/"],["sedentary","ít vận động, chủ yếu ngồi một chỗ","a sedentary lifestyle","Health","A sedentary lifestyle can harm your health.","/ˈsed.ən.tər.i/"],["rectify","sửa chữa hoặc khắc phục lỗi sai","rectify a mistake / problem","English Learning Challenges","I tried to rectify my mistakes, but I kept making them.","/ˈrek.tɪ.faɪ/"],["err","mắc lỗi, làm sai","err by doing something","English Learning Challenges","I kept erring by choosing inappropriate words and going off topic.","/ɜːr/"],["not do someone any favours","không có lợi cho ai, gây bất lợi cho ai","not do yourself any favours","English Learning Challenges","Using complicated words that do not fit the topic will not do you any favours.","/nɒt duː ˈsʌmwʌn ˈɛni ˈfeɪvəz/"],["pertinent","thích hợp và liên quan trực tiếp đến chủ đề","pertinent to the topic","English Learning Challenges","I try to use only vocabulary that is pertinent to the topic.","/ˈpɜː.tɪ.nənt/"],["steer clear of","tránh xa, không dùng hoặc không tiếp xúc với","steer clear of something","English Learning Challenges","I steer clear of fancy but unnecessary words in my writing.","/stɪə klɪər ɒv/"]]);
words.push(...[["come to terms with sth","Dần chấp nhận một sự thật hoặc tình huống khó khăn","come to terms with a failure / change / the fact that…; it took me a while to come to terms with it","Universal Words","My last IELTS speaking score was lower than I had hoped, and it took me a few days to come to terms with it before I could plan my retake. Older villagers are still coming to terms with the apartment blocks replacing the fields they grew up with."],["set sb back + amount","Khiến ai đó tốn một khoản tiền","It set me back + amount; How much did it set you back?; It won’t set you back much.","Universal Words","A new iPhone would set me back more than two months of my Uniqlo wages, so I’m holding on to my old one. If you book early, a return flight to Taiwan doesn’t have to set you back that much."],["on the spur of the moment","Tùy hứng, không có kế hoạch từ trước","do sth on the spur of the moment; a spur-of-the-moment decision / trip","Universal Words","Some of my best food tours happened on the spur of the moment. Most of the clothes I never wear were things I bought on the spur of the moment during a flash sale."],["go out of your way to + V","Cố gắng, chịu khó làm gì dù không bắt buộc","go out of my / her / their way to help or do sth; go out of your way for sb","Universal Words","Good hotel staff go out of their way to make guests feel welcome. In Vietnamese villages, neighbours often go out of their way to help when a family has a wedding or funeral."],["be in (high / great) demand","Được săn đón, có nhu cầu cao","be in high / great demand; meet the demand for sth; demand for sth is growing","Universal Words","Tour guides who speak Russian are in high demand in places like Nha Trang. Since AI took off, people with strong digital skills have been in great demand."],["have a lot / little / nothing to do with sth","Có liên quan nhiều / ít / không liên quan đến điều gì","It has nothing to do with…; I think it has something to do with…; have a lot to do with…","Universal Words","My tiredness has a lot to do with staying up late doing homework, not with my job. The heavy floods in recent years have a lot to do with cities spreading onto land that used to soak up rainwater."]]);
words.push(...[["travel solo / go it alone / there’s safety in numbers","Đi du lịch một mình / tự làm một mình / đi đông thì an toàn hơn","travel solo for the first time; solo travel is becoming more popular; I’d never go it alone; travel in a group because there’s safety in numbers","Travel & Transport","Solo travel sounds exciting, but I’d rather not go it alone, at least not yet. When I travel with friends, I feel safer, because there’s safety in numbers."],["plan sth down to the last detail / go with the flow","Lên kế hoạch kỹ đến từng chi tiết / thuận theo tình hình, không lên kế hoạch cứng nhắc","plan the whole trip down to the last detail; have every detail planned; just go with the flow; learn to go with the flow","Travel & Transport","My friend plans every trip down to the last detail, even where we’ll eat each meal, whereas I prefer to book a room in advance and then just go with the flow."],["travel on a shoestring / a budget airline","Du lịch với ngân sách rất hạn chế / hãng hàng không giá rẻ","travel on a shoestring; a holiday on a shoestring budget; fly with a budget airline; budget airlines make travel more affordable","Travel & Transport","When I was a student, my friends and I travelled to Đà Lạt on a shoestring. We flew with a budget airline and stayed in a cheap homestay, so the trip didn’t break the bank."],["hit the road / go on a road trip","Lên đường, xuất phát / đi một chuyến dài bằng xe máy hoặc ô tô","hit the road early in the morning; it’s time to hit the road; go on a road trip to Hà Giang; a two-day road trip","Travel & Transport","Last year, my friends and I went on a road trip to Ninh Bình by motorbike. We hit the road at five in the morning to avoid the traffic."],["weave in and out of traffic / ride a motorbike","Lạng lách, luồn lách qua dòng xe / đi xe máy","motorbikes weave in and out of traffic; ride a motorbike to work/university; ride on the back of a motorbike; motorbikes are the main way to get around","Travel & Transport","In Hanoi, most people ride motorbikes because they can weave in and out of traffic during rush hour. I used to ride forty kilometres a day to university."],["go for a stroll / stretch my legs / pedestrian-friendly","Đi dạo thong thả / đi lại cho giãn chân / thân thiện với người đi bộ","go for a stroll around Hoàn Kiếm Lake; get out of the car to stretch my legs; a pedestrian-friendly street/city","Travel & Transport","Hanoi isn’t very pedestrian-friendly, because the pavements are full of parked motorbikes. But at the weekend I love going for a stroll around Hoàn Kiếm Lake."],["go through security / a layover / miss my flight","Qua cửa kiểm tra an ninh / chặng dừng nối chuyến / lỡ chuyến bay","queue to go through security; a three-hour layover in Bangkok; nearly miss my flight; get to the airport early","Travel & Transport","On my first trip to Ho Chi Minh City, the queue to go through security was so long that I nearly missed my flight."],["pick up a souvenir / something to remember the trip by","Mua một món quà lưu niệm / một thứ để nhớ về chuyến đi","pick up a few souvenirs for my family; a souvenir from Sa Pa; buy something to remember it by; a tacky souvenir","Travel & Transport","I never buy fridge magnets, but I always pick up something small to remember the trip by. In Hội An, I bought a silk scarf."],["put a strain on sth / overtourism","Gây áp lực, làm quá tải cho thứ gì / tình trạng quá tải du lịch","put a strain on local roads/water supplies/the environment; overtourism in Hạ Long Bay/Sa Pa; tourism brings in money, but…","Travel & Transport","Tourism brings a lot of money into places like Sa Pa, but overtourism can put a strain on local roads and water supplies, and it changes the local way of life."],["It’s only a matter of time before + present simple","Chỉ là vấn đề thời gian trước khi…; sớm muộn gì cũng…","before electric motorbikes replace petrol ones; before self-driving cars become common; before the metro reaches the suburbs","Travel & Transport","I think it’s only a matter of time before most people in Hanoi switch to electric motorbikes, because they’re cheaper to run and better for the air."]]);words.push(...[["a game-changer / do the heavy lifting","Thứ làm thay đổi hoàn toàn cách làm việc / gánh phần việc nặng nhọc nhất","a game-changer for sth; do the heavy lifting; let a tool do the heavy lifting","Technology & Media","AI tools have been a game-changer for my IELTS writing practice. They do the heavy lifting of spotting grammar mistakes, but I still have to work out how to fix them myself."],["put (people) out of work / automate","Khiến ai đó mất việc / tự động hoá","automate repetitive jobs; put workers out of work; jobs are at risk of being automated","Technology & Media","AI will probably automate repetitive jobs like data entry, and that could put a lot of office workers out of work."],["jump on a video call / you're on mute / the screen keeps freezing","Tham gia cuộc gọi video nhanh / bạn đang tắt mic / màn hình liên tục bị đơ","jump on a quick video call; you're on mute; the screen keeps freezing; have a poor connection","Technology & Media","During online classes at university, the screen kept freezing, and once I talked for two minutes before I realised I was on mute."],["hide behind a screen / cyberbullying","Núp sau màn hình / bắt nạt trên mạng","hide behind a screen; experience cyberbullying; leave nasty comments online","Technology & Media","People are ruder online because they can hide behind a screen. They leave nasty comments they would never say face to face."],["be bombarded with ads / targeted ads","Bị quảng cáo dội tới tấp / quảng cáo nhắm mục tiêu","be bombarded with ads; targeted ads based on your searches; see ads for sth","Technology & Media","Once I searched for a lace top on Shopee, and for the next week I was bombarded with ads for lace tops."],["on its last legs / upgrade to (the latest model)","Sắp hỏng / nâng cấp lên mẫu mới","my phone is on its last legs; upgrade to the latest model; upgrade to a newer model","Technology & Media","My phone is four years old and it's on its last legs. I'll upgrade to a newer model."],["strain my eyes / the feel of a real book","Làm mỏi mắt / cảm giác cầm một cuốn sách giấy thật","reading on a screen strains my eyes; love the feel of a real book; read for long periods","Technology & Media","Reading on a screen for a long time strains my eyes. I just love the feel of a real book in my hands."],["put a filter on (a photo) / unrealistic beauty standards","Dùng bộ lọc chỉnh ảnh / những tiêu chuẩn vẻ đẹp phi thực tế","put a filter on a photo; heavily edited photos; create unrealistic beauty standards","Technology & Media","Many photos are heavily edited, and I think that creates unrealistic beauty standards for young women."],["set up parental controls / inappropriate content","Cài đặt chế độ kiểm soát của phụ huynh / nội dung không phù hợp","set up parental controls on a tablet; protect children from inappropriate content; parental control settings","Technology & Media","Parents should set up parental controls on their children's tablets to protect them from inappropriate content."],["With the rise of + noun, …","Cùng với sự phát triển / lên ngôi của…","with the rise of streaming platforms; with the rise of social media; with the rise of + noun","Technology & Media","With the rise of streaming platforms like Netflix, fewer young people watch TV at a fixed time."],["have green fingers / houseplants","Có tay trồng cây / cây cảnh trồng trong nhà","my mum has green fingers; I don't have green fingers at all; keep a few houseplants on my desk; water the plants","Nature & Environment","My mum really has green fingers. Our yard is full of flowers and herbs. I'm the opposite, though. I've tried keeping a few houseplants in my room, but they all died within a month."],["keep a pet / a big commitment","Nuôi thú cưng / một trách nhiệm lớn cần nhiều thời gian và công sức","keep a cat/dog; having a pet is a big commitment; take the dog for a walk; pets need time and attention","Nature & Environment","I'd love to keep a cat one day, but having a pet is a big commitment. You have to feed it, clean up after it and take it to the vet, so I'll wait until I have a stable job."],["run dry / be in short supply","Cạn khô / khan hiếm, không đủ dùng","rivers/wells/taps run dry; clean water is in short supply; fresh water is becoming scarce","Nature & Environment","In some parts of the Mekong Delta, clean water is in short supply during the dry season, and some rivers almost run dry. It makes me realise I take running water for granted."],["renewable energy / move away from fossil fuels","Năng lượng tái tạo / dần bỏ nhiên liệu hoá thạch như than, dầu, khí","invest in renewable energy; solar/wind power; install solar panels; move away from coal; cut our reliance on fossil fuels","Nature & Environment","I think Vietnam should invest more in renewable energy, like solar power, and gradually move away from coal. It's expensive at first, but it's much better for the air in the long run."],["to make way for (+ noun) / deforestation","Nhường chỗ cho thứ khác / nạn phá rừng","forests are cut down to make way for farms/roads/resorts; deforestation leads to habitat loss and floods","Nature & Environment","Near where I live, a lot of rice fields were cleared to make way for new apartment blocks. On a bigger scale, deforestation in the mountains makes floods and landslides much worse."],["in captivity / in the wild","Bị nuôi nhốt / ngoài tự nhiên hoang dã","animals kept/born in captivity; survive in the wild; see animals in their natural habitat","Nature & Environment","I have mixed feelings about zoos. Animals in captivity often look bored and stressed, but some zoos help protect endangered species that could die out in the wild."],["light pollution / gaze up at the stars","Ô nhiễm ánh sáng / ngước nhìn ngắm sao","because of light pollution; on a clear night; a starry sky; lie on the grass and gaze up at the stars","Nature & Environment","In the city centre you can barely see any stars because of light pollution. But in the countryside, on a clear night, you can lie on a mat and gaze up at a sky full of stars."],["lead by example / from a young age","Làm gương / từ khi còn nhỏ","parents should lead by example; teach children to recycle from a young age; get into good habits early","Nature & Environment","I think children should learn about the environment from a young age, but lessons at school aren't enough. Parents need to lead by example, because kids copy what they see at home, not what they're told."],["leave no trace / eco-tourism","Không để lại dấu vết, chẳng hạn rác / du lịch sinh thái có trách nhiệm","take your rubbish with you and leave no trace; eco-tourism is becoming more popular; stay in a local homestay; protect unspoilt areas","Nature & Environment","Eco-tourism is a great idea, as long as it's done properly. When I go somewhere beautiful, I try to take my rubbish home and leave no trace, so the place stays unspoilt for the next visitors."],["Both X and Y have a role to play in + -ing","Cả X và Y đều có vai trò, đều có phần trách nhiệm trong việc…","both the government and individuals have a role to play in protecting the environment; schools also have a role to play; everyone has a part to play","Nature & Environment","I think both the government and individuals have a role to play in protecting the environment. The government can invest in renewable energy, while ordinary people can cut down on single-use plastic."],["tie the knot / a traditional wedding","Kết hôn (thân mật) / đám cưới truyền thống","tie the knot next year; a wedding ceremony; the bride and groom; invite the whole village; a wedding banquet","Culture & Society","My cousin is going to tie the knot next spring. She wants a traditional wedding, with the family procession and the tea ceremony, so the whole family is busy preparing."],["pay respects (to) / burn incense","Bày tỏ lòng kính trọng / thắp hương","pay respects to our ancestors; burn incense at the family altar; visit a pagoda; make offerings; on the anniversary of someone's death","Culture & Society","On the first day of Tết, my family always burns incense at the altar to pay respects to our ancestors. It's a custom that is deeply rooted in Vietnamese culture."],["make the most of (a long weekend) / a public holiday","Tận dụng tối đa / ngày nghỉ lễ chung của cả nước","make the most of the long weekend; a four-day weekend; head back to my hometown; get a day off","Culture & Society","On public holidays, many people in Hanoi head out of the city, but I usually stay home with my family. We make the most of the time by cooking together and catching up."],["a heritage site / fall into disrepair","Di tích, di sản / xuống cấp vì không được tu sửa","a UNESCO World Heritage Site; preserve our heritage; restore old buildings; fall into disrepair; a source of national pride","Culture & Society","Many old houses in the Old Quarter have fallen into disrepair. I think the government should restore them, because heritage sites help young people understand where they come from."],["petty crime / keep an eye on (my belongings)","Tội phạm vặt / để mắt trông chừng","petty crime like bag snatching; feel safe walking at night; keep an eye on my bag; take precautions","Culture & Society","My neighbourhood is quite safe. There's a little petty crime in crowded places, so I always keep an eye on my belongings in the market."],["the cost of living / make ends meet","Chi phí sinh hoạt / xoay xở đủ tiền sống","the cost of living is rising; struggle to make ends meet; live on a tight budget; take a part-time job to cover expenses","Culture & Society","The cost of living in Hanoi has risen sharply. Many young people have to take a part-time job just to make ends meet."],["sky-high rents / get on the property ladder","Giá thuê nhà cao ngất / mua được căn nhà đầu tiên","rents are sky-high; house prices keep rising; save for a deposit; get on the property ladder; afford a place of my own","Culture & Society","With rents so sky-high in the city centre, it's very hard for people my age to get on the property ladder. Many of us stay with our parents for years."],["jump the queue / push in","Chen ngang hàng / chen vào giữa hàng","someone jumped the queue; push in front of me; wait my turn; be polite in public","Culture & Society","What really annoys me is when someone jumps the queue at the supermarket. In some places it's frowned upon, but here people just push in without saying sorry."],["(be) part and parcel of (+ noun)","Là một phần không thể tách rời, một mặt tất yếu của việc gì đó","part and parcel of modern life/city life/growing up/the job","Culture & Society","Social media is now part and parcel of everyday life. It connects people, but constant comparison and online pressure come with it."],["Opinions are divided on whether ...","Ý kiến chia rẽ về việc liệu…","opinions are divided on whether…; some people argue that…, while others believe that…; personally, I lean towards…","Culture & Society","Opinions are divided on whether historic buildings should be preserved. Some people argue that they cost too much, while others believe they are part of our identity. Personally, I lean towards preserving them."]]);
words.push(...[
["take a toll on sth / sb","Gây tổn hại dần dần đến điều gì / ai","take a toll on your health / sleep / mood / the environment; take a heavy / serious toll on sth (say “a toll on”, not “a toll to”)","Universal Words","Staying up late doing homework has really taken a toll on my sleep, and I can feel it in my mood the next day. Years of air pollution have taken a heavy toll on people's health in big cities like Hanoi.","/ˌteɪk ə ˈtəʊl ɒn/"],
["get out of the habit of + V-ing","Bỏ một thói quen","get out of the habit of doing sth; get out of the habit of checking / buying / staying up late","Universal Words","I'm trying to get out of the habit of checking shopping apps every night, because that's when I spend the most."],
["get into the habit of + V-ing","Tạo thành một thói quen","get into the habit of doing sth","Universal Words","Since I started working at Uniqlo, I've got into the habit of doing one reading passage during my break."],
["hold sb back","Cản trở, kìm hãm ai","hold sb back from + V-ing; What's holding you back? (say “hold me back”, not “hold back me”)","Universal Words","My weak grammar under pressure is the main thing holding me back in the speaking test. High rents hold a lot of young people back from moving to big cities."],
["have mixed feelings about sth","Có cảm xúc lẫn lộn; vừa thích vừa không","have mixed feelings about sth; I have mixed feelings (always plural)","Universal Words","I have mixed feelings about my village changing. The new supermarket is convenient, but I miss the fields. I also have mixed feelings about short videos: they're fun, but they hurt my attention span."],
["put pressure on sb / be under pressure","Gây áp lực lên ai / chịu áp lực","put pressure on sb to + V; be under pressure to + V (say “under pressure”, not “in pressure”)","Universal Words","Relatives sometimes put pressure on young people to find a stable job after graduating. Mass tourism puts pressure on local water supplies."],
["go downhill","Tệ dần đi","sth has gone downhill since…; go downhill fast","Universal Words","That restaurant used to be my favourite, but the food has really gone downhill since it got popular. When I stay up late, my concentration goes downhill the next day."],
["more often than not","Thường thì, phần lớn trường hợp","more often than not, + clause; a natural alternative to “usually”","Universal Words","More often than not, the clothes I buy online don't fit, so I try them on in the shop first. I also get stuck in traffic on my way into the city."],
["I've never really thought about it, but I suppose / I'd say…","Thật ra mình chưa nghĩ đến chuyện này, nhưng chắc là…","Use as a short opener in Part 3, then give a direct answer; use it once or twice, not as a substitute for an answer.","Universal Words","I've never really thought about it, but I suppose young people face more pressure today. More often than not, they compare themselves with others online, and fear of failing can hold them back from trying new things."]
]);
const universalRefresh={
"a far cry from":{"meaning":"Khác xa so với…","pattern":"a far cry from + noun","topic":"Universal Words","example":"A clean, air-conditioned restaurant is a far cry from plastic stools by the road, but the street food often tastes better."},
"keep up with something":{"meaning":"Theo kịp, bắt kịp","pattern":"keep up with + noun","topic":"Universal Words","example":"Between my Russian classes and my shifts, I sometimes find it hard to keep up with my IELTS practice."},
"strike a balance between A and B":{"meaning":"Cân bằng giữa hai điều","pattern":"strike a balance between A and B","topic":"Universal Words","example":"Students who work part-time need to strike a balance between earning money and keeping their grades up."},
"end up doing something":{"meaning":"Cuối cùng lại làm gì ngoài dự định","pattern":"end up + V-ing","topic":"Universal Words","example":"If I can't find a good job in Hanoi, I might end up moving to Nha Trang to work in tourism."}
};
for(const entry of words){const update=universalRefresh[entry[0]];if(update){entry[1]=update.meaning;entry[2]=update.pattern;entry[3]=update.topic;entry[4]=update.example}}
const wordKey=text=>String(text||'').toLowerCase().normalize('NFKD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').replace(/[’‘]/g,"'").replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,' ').trim();{const unique=[],byKey=new Map();for(const row of words){const key=wordKey(row[0]),existing=byKey.get(key);if(!existing){byKey.set(key,row);unique.push(row)}else{for(let i=1;i<row.length;i++)if(!existing[i]&&row[i])existing[i]=row[i]}}words.splice(0,words.length,...unique)}const topics=[...new Set(words.map(w=>w[3]))], $=s=>document.querySelector(s);const translationTasks=[{"topic":"Daily Life","vi":"Thực sự chả có gì đặc biệt về việc tôi bắt đầu ngày mới của mình.","en":"There is really nothing special about how I go about my day."},{"topic":"Daily Life","vi":"Thường thường tôi sẽ thức dậy vào 7 giờ sáng hoặc gần thời điểm đó, đánh răng, rửa mặt, và chuẩn bị đến trường.","en":"I usually wake up at around 7 a.m., brush my teeth, wash my face, and get ready for school."},{"topic":"Daily Life","vi":"Tôi không quá cầu kì về ngoại hình của mình, nên thường thì quần áo bình thường và thoải mái là tất cả những gì tôi cần.","en":"I'm not fussy about my appearance, so casual, comfortable clothes are all I need."},{"topic":"Daily Life","vi":"Một bữa sáng đầy đủ là một sự xa xỉ đối với tôi; tôi chỉ có đủ thời gian để tìm một ít đồ ăn nhanh tại cửa hàng tiện lợi bên cạnh phòng trọ trước khi chạy đi bắt xe buýt đến trường đại học.","en":"A proper breakfast is a luxury for me; I only have time to grab a bite to eat at the convenience store next to my rented room before running to catch the bus bound for the university."},{"topic":"Daily Life","vi":"Lúc tầm 12 giờ trưa, những bài học buổi sáng đã kết thúc, và tôi có vừa đủ thời gian để về nhà, ăn trưa, và ngay sau đó hối hả đến trường học tiếp buổi chiều.","en":"Around noon, my morning classes end, and I have just enough time to go home, have lunch, and rush straight back for the afternoon session."},{"topic":"Daily Life","vi":"Sau khi tan học, tôi trở về phòng để làm việc nhà, tắm rửa, và ăn tối với những người bạn cùng phòng.","en":"After class, I go back to my room to do chores, take a shower, and have dinner with my roommates."},{"topic":"Daily Life","vi":"Tiếp theo, tôi giải quyết công việc của mình trong xấp xỉ 3 tiếng.","en":"Then, I work on my own tasks for about three hours."},{"topic":"Daily Life","vi":"Buổi tối của tôi kết thúc vào khoảng 12 giờ tối, lúc mà tôi lên giường đi ngủ và sẵn sàng để làm lại tất cả vào ngày tiếp theo.","en":"My evening ends around midnight, when I hit the hay and get ready to do it all over again the next day."},{"topic":"Weekend","vi":"Tôi thường xuyên bị nhấn chìm trong công việc vào giữa tuần, nên tôi luôn háo hức đến cuối tuần.","en":"I'm often snowed under with work in the middle of the week, so I always look forward to the weekend."},{"topic":"Weekend","vi":"Không phải bao giờ tôi cũng đi chơi - đôi khi tôi không muốn gì hơn ngoài đắp những lớp chăn ấm lên mình trên giường và nghỉ ngơi cho xứng đáng.","en":"I don't always go out – sometimes I want nothing more than to bundle up in bed and take a well-deserved rest."},{"topic":"Weekend","vi":"Tuy vậy, tôi yêu thích việc thỉnh thoảng tự đãi mình một chuyến đi đặc biệt.","en":"Still, I love treating myself to a special trip now and then."},{"topic":"Weekend","vi":"Một trong những nơi \"tủ\" của tôi chính là cái sở thú mà tôi đi qua hằng ngày trên đường đến trường.","en":"One of my favourite spots is the zoo I pass every day on my way to school."},{"topic":"Weekend","vi":"Vào những ngày trong tuần, tôi chỉ có thể ngắm một cách thèm muốn vào nơi được phủ xanh bởi cây cối um tùm, luôn đầy người lớn và trẻ con.","en":"On weekdays, I can only gaze longingly at the lush, green place that is always swarming with adults and children."},{"topic":"Weekend","vi":"Nhưng khi rảnh, thứ bảy và chủ nhật chẳng hạn, tôi có thể trải nghiệm cảm giác hạnh phúc khi tới thăm và nhìn cảnh vật với sự ngưỡng mộ.","en":"But when I'm free, on Saturdays and Sundays for example, I can enjoy the happiness of visiting and admiring the scenery."},{"topic":"Weekend","vi":"Đi cùng tôi là bạn bè, và chúng tôi đi lang thang và có khoảng thời gian vui vẻ.","en":"My friends come along, and we wander around and have a great time."},{"topic":"Weekend","vi":"Đây là một cách tốt tiếp thêm sinh lực và nâng cao tinh thần trong những ngày nghỉ.","en":"It's a great, uplifting way to recharge on days off."},{"topic":"Travel","vi":"Gia đình tôi không khá giả cho lắm, nên chúng tôi hiếm khi đi du lịch xa.","en":"My family isn't very well-off, so we rarely travel far."},{"topic":"Travel","vi":"Thay vào đó, chúng tôi nhảy lên xe máy và hướng tới những địa điểm hút khách du lịch gần nhất, và chỗ tôi thích nhất là biển Thạch Hải.","en":"Instead, we hop on our motorbikes and head to the nearest tourist spots, and my favourite is Thach Hai beach."},{"topic":"Travel","vi":"Nó khá ẩn mình, ít người biết đến, nhưng rất xinh đẹp, với cát vàng ươm và những đợt sóng nhẹ nhàng vỗ vào bờ.","en":"It's a fairly obscure place, but it's beautiful, with golden sand and gentle waves lapping the shore."},{"topic":"Travel","vi":"Chính vì thế, dù ít tiếng tăm, từng đoàn người vẫn lũ lượt kéo đến mỗi ngày.","en":"Because of that, despite its relative anonymity, hordes of people still flock there every day."},{"topic":"Travel","vi":"Tôi nhớ lần đầu cha mẹ đưa tôi đến đây; sáng tinh mơ tôi đã bị đánh thức và chở đến bãi biển.","en":"I remember the first time my parents took me there; I was woken up in the wee hours and driven to the beach."},{"topic":"Travel","vi":"Tôi vẫn nửa tỉnh nửa mê, khó chịu vì phải dậy sớm, thì một màu xanh mênh mông thành hình trước mắt và chiếm trọn sự chú ý của tôi.","en":"I was still half asleep and annoyed at getting up so early when a vast blue expanse took shape before my eyes and captured my full attention."},{"topic":"Travel","vi":"Trong thoáng chốc, bình minh lên, và khi tia sáng đầu tiên chạm lên mặt tôi, tôi hoàn toàn tỉnh táo và sững sờ trước cảnh vật.","en":"In a moment, the sun rose, and when the first ray touched my face, I was wide awake and transfixed by the view."},{"topic":"Travel","vi":"Đó thực sự là một khoảnh khắc kỳ diệu.","en":"It was truly a magical moment."},{"topic":"Memorable Experiences","vi":"4 năm trước, tôi đã dấn thân vào một chuyến đi đến Hà Nội với một số con người vô cùng đặc biệt.","en":"Four years ago, I embarked on a trip to Hanoi with some very special people."},{"topic":"Memorable Experiences","vi":"Đó là năm 2019 - trước đó tôi đã vào được đội tuyển quốc gia Tiếng Anh của tỉnh và vô cùng kinh ngạc khi thấy những người tôi khâm phục - các anh chị lớn tuổi và rất giỏi - cùng học một phòng với tôi trong 5 tháng tiếp theo.","en":"It was 2019 – I had made my province's national English team and was star-struck to see people I deeply admired – older, brilliant seniors – studying in the same room as me for the next five months."},{"topic":"Memorable Experiences","vi":"Vì vậy hãy thử tưởng tượng niềm vui sướng vô bờ của tôi khi biết chúng tôi có cơ hội đi Hà Nội học và vui chơi cùng nhau ở thành phố lớn.","en":"So imagine my glee when I learned we had the chance to go to Hanoi for classes and to have fun together in the big city."},{"topic":"Memorable Experiences","vi":"Chuyến đi đã không hề làm tôi thất vọng.","en":"The trip didn't disappoint me."},{"topic":"Memorable Experiences","vi":"Tôi được học những bài học thú vị nhất từng thấy, đồng thời tương tác, kết nối với những anh hùng của mình và trở thành bạn của họ.","en":"I attended the most interesting lessons I'd ever had, and got to interact and bond with my heroes and become their friend."},{"topic":"Memorable Experiences","vi":"Chúng tôi nói về bất kì chủ đề nào có thể, từ những câu chuyện đời tư đến
